<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <meta name="description" content="Server Dashboard for monitoring and controlling the CV Navigation System">
    <title>Server Dashboard - CV Navigation System</title>
    <style>
        @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Poppins:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800;900&display=swap');

        :root {
            --primary-gradient: linear-gradient(135deg, #6366f1 0%, #8b5cf6 25%, #d946ef 50%, #f59e0b 75%, #ef4444 100%);
            --secondary-gradient: linear-gradient(135deg, #1e293b 0%, #334155 100%);
            --accent-gradient: linear-gradient(135deg, #06b6d4 0%, #3b82f6 100%);
            --glass-bg: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
            --glass-border: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
            --text-primary: #ffffff;
            --text-secondary: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8);
            --shadow-primary: 0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
            --shadow-glow: 0 0 40px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3);
        }

        * {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        body {
            font-family: 'Poppins', sans-serif;
            background: #0f0f23;
            min-height: 100vh;
            overflow-x: hidden;
            position: relative;
            padding: 20px;
        }

        body::before {
            content: '';
            position: fixed;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background: var(--primary-gradient);
            opacity: 0.1;
            z-index: 1;
        }

        .container {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 10;
            max-width: 1400px;
            margin: 0 auto;
        }

        .header {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 20px;
            padding: 30px;
            margin-bottom: 30px;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-primary);
        }

        .header h1 {
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-size: 2.5rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            background: var(--primary-gradient);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            background-clip: text;
        }

        .header p {
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: 1rem;
        }

        .nav-links {
            margin-top: 15px;
        }

        .nav-links a {
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            text-decoration: none;
            margin-right: 20px;
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            transition: color 0.3s;
        }

        .nav-links a:hover {
            color: var(--text-primary);
        }

        /* View Switcher */
        .view-switcher {
            display: flex;
            gap: 10px;
            margin-top: 15px;
            padding: 5px;
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
            border-radius: 10px;
            width: fit-content;
        }

        .view-switcher button {
            padding: 10px 20px;
            border: none;
            border-radius: 8px;
            background: transparent;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            font-weight: 500;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.3s;
            font-family: 'Poppins', sans-serif;
        }

        .view-switcher button.active {
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            color: white;
        }

        .view-switcher button:hover:not(.active) {
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
            color: var(--text-primary);
        }

        /* Camera Grid */
        .camera-grid {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(350px, 1fr));
            gap: 20px;
            margin-bottom: 30px;
        }

        .camera-card {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 15px;
            padding: 20px;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-primary);
            transition: transform 0.3s, box-shadow 0.3s;
            cursor: pointer;
        }

        .camera-card:hover {
            transform: translateY(-5px);
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-glow);
        }

        .camera-card.offline {
            opacity: 0.6;
            border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
        }

        .camera-header {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
        }

        .camera-title {
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-size: 1.1rem;
            font-weight: 600;
        }

        .camera-status-dot {
            width: 12px;
            height: 12px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: #4ade80;
            box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(74, 222, 128, 0.5);
        }

        .camera-status-dot.offline {
            background: #ef4444;
            box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.5);
        }

        .camera-info {
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }

        .camera-info-row {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            padding: 5px 0;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: 0.85rem;
        }

        .camera-info-row .value {
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-weight: 500;
        }

        .camera-actions {
            display: flex;
            gap: 8px;
            margin-top: 15px;
        }

        .camera-btn {
            flex: 1;
            padding: 8px 12px;
            border: none;
            border-radius: 8px;
            font-size: 0.85rem;
            font-weight: 500;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.2s;
            font-family: 'Poppins', sans-serif;
        }

        .camera-btn-message {
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            color: white;
        }

        .camera-btn-message:hover {
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.3);
        }

        .camera-btn-details {
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
            color: var(--text-primary);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
        }

        .camera-btn-details:hover {
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
        }

        .camera-btn-view {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #10b981 0%, #059669 100%);
            color: white;
        }

        .camera-btn-view:hover {
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3);
        }

        .camera-btn-voice {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #06b6d4 0%, #3b82f6 100%);
            color: white;
        }

        .camera-btn-voice:hover {
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.3);
        }

        /* Modal */
        .modal {
            display: none;
            position: fixed;
            z-index: 1000;
            left: 0;
            top: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
            backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
        }

        .modal.active {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
        }

        .modal-content {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 20px;
            padding: 30px;
            max-width: 600px;
            width: 90%;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-primary);
        }

        .modal-header {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
        }

        .modal-title {
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-size: 1.5rem;
            font-weight: 600;
        }

        .modal-close {
            background: none;
            border: none;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: 1.5rem;
            cursor: pointer;
            padding: 5px 10px;
            transition: color 0.3s;
        }

        .modal-close:hover {
            color: var(--text-primary);
        }

        .message-input {
            width: 100%;
            padding: 12px;
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 10px;
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-family: 'Poppins', sans-serif;
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            resize: vertical;
            min-height: 100px;
        }

        .message-input:focus {
            outline: none;
            border-color: #06b6d4;
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
        }

        .empty-state {
            text-align: center;
            padding: 60px 20px;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
        }

        .empty-state-icon {
            font-size: 3rem;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            opacity: 0.5;
        }

        .camera-stats {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
            gap: 15px;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
        }

        .camera-stat-card {
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
            border-radius: 10px;
            padding: 15px;
            text-align: center;
        }

        .camera-stat-value {
            font-size: 1.5rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: var(--text-primary);
            margin-bottom: 5px;
        }

        .camera-stat-label {
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
        }

        .metrics-grid {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
            gap: 20px;
            margin-bottom: 30px;
        }

        .metric-card {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 15px;
            padding: 25px;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-primary);
            transition: transform 0.3s, box-shadow 0.3s;
        }

        .metric-card:hover {
            transform: translateY(-5px);
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-glow);
        }

        .metric-card h3 {
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-size: 1.1rem;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            font-weight: 600;
        }

        .metric-value {
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-size: 2.5rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            background-clip: text;
        }

        .metric-label {
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: 0.85rem;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
        }

        .metric-bar {
            height: 8px;
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
            border-radius: 4px;
            overflow: hidden;
            margin-top: 10px;
        }

        .metric-bar-fill {
            height: 100%;
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            transition: width 0.5s ease;
            border-radius: 4px;
        }

        .status-badge {
            display: inline-block;
            padding: 5px 15px;
            border-radius: 20px;
            font-size: 0.85rem;
            font-weight: 500;
            margin-top: 10px;
        }

        .status-healthy {
            background: rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.2);
            color: #4ade80;
            border: 1px solid rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.3);
        }

        .status-warning {
            background: rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.2);
            color: #fb923c;
            border: 1px solid rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.3);
        }

        .status-error {
            background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2);
            color: #ef4444;
            border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
        }

        .control-panel {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 15px;
            padding: 25px;
            margin-bottom: 30px;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-primary);
        }

        .control-panel h2 {
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-size: 1.5rem;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            font-weight: 600;
        }

        .button-group {
            display: flex;
            flex-wrap: wrap;
            gap: 15px;
        }

        .btn {
            padding: 12px 25px;
            border: none;
            border-radius: 10px;
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.3s;
            font-family: 'Poppins', sans-serif;
        }

        .btn-primary {
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            color: white;
        }

        .btn-primary:hover {
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 10px 20px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.3);
        }

        .btn-danger {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ef4444 0%, #dc2626 100%);
            color: white;
        }

        .btn-danger:hover {
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 10px 20px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
        }

        .btn-secondary {
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
            color: var(--text-primary);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
        }

        .btn-secondary:hover {
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
        }

        .btn:disabled {
            opacity: 0.5;
            cursor: not-allowed;
        }

        .warnings {
            background: rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.1);
            border: 1px solid rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.3);
            border-radius: 10px;
            padding: 15px;
            margin-top: 20px;
        }

        .warnings h4 {
            color: #fb923c;
            font-size: 1rem;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }

        .warnings ul {
            list-style: none;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: 0.9rem;
        }

        .warnings li {
            padding: 5px 0;
        }

        .warnings li::before {
            content: '⚠ ';
            margin-right: 5px;
        }

        .last-update {
            text-align: center;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: 0.85rem;
            margin-top: 20px;
        }

        .loading {
            text-align: center;
            padding: 50px;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
        }

        .spinner {
            border: 3px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
            border-top: 3px solid #6366f1;
            border-radius: 50%;
            width: 40px;
            height: 40px;
            animation: spin 1s linear infinite;
            margin: 0 auto 20px;
        }

        @keyframes spin {
            0% { transform: rotate(0deg); }
            100% { transform: rotate(360deg); }
        }

        .detail-row {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            padding: 8px 0;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: 0.9rem;
        }

        .detail-row:last-child {
            border-bottom: none;
        }

        .detail-row .label {
            font-weight: 500;
        }

        .detail-row .value {
            color: var(--text-primary);
        }

        @media (max-width: 768px) {
            .metrics-grid {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }

            .header h1 {
                font-size: 1.8rem;
            }

            .button-group {
                flex-direction: column;
            }

            .btn {
                width: 100%;
            }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="header">
            <h1>Server Dashboard</h1>
            <p>Monitor and control your CV Navigation System</p>
            <div class="nav-links">
                <a href="/">Main Application</a>
                <a href="#" onclick="refreshMetrics()">Refresh</a>
            </div>
            <div class="view-switcher">
                <button id="serverHealthBtn" class="active" onclick="switchView('server-health')">Server Health</button>
                <button id="cameraManagementBtn" onclick="switchView('camera-management')">Camera Management</button>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="loadingIndicator" class="loading">
            <div class="spinner"></div>
            <p>Loading metrics...</p>
        </div>

        <div id="dashboardContent" style="display: none;">
            <!-- Health Status -->
            <div class="control-panel">
                <h2>System Health</h2>
                <div id="healthStatus">
                    <div class="detail-row">
                        <span class="label">Status:</span>
                        <span class="value" id="overallStatus">-</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-row">
                        <span class="label">Uptime:</span>
                        <span class="value" id="uptime">-</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-row">
                        <span class="label">Last Updated:</span>
                        <span class="value" id="lastUpdate">-</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div id="warningsContainer"></div>
            </div>

            <!-- Metrics Grid -->
            <div class="metrics-grid">
                <!-- CPU -->
                <div class="metric-card">
                    <h3>CPU Usage</h3>
                    <div class="metric-value" id="cpuValue">0%</div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Cores: <span id="cpuCores">0</span></div>
                    <div class="metric-bar">
                        <div class="metric-bar-fill" id="cpuBar" style="width: 0%"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Memory -->
                <div class="metric-card">
                    <h3>Memory Usage</h3>
                    <div class="metric-value" id="memoryValue">0%</div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Used: <span id="memoryUsed">0</span> / <span id="memoryTotal">0</span> GB</div>
                    <div class="metric-bar">
                        <div class="metric-bar-fill" id="memoryBar" style="width: 0%"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- GPU -->
                <div class="metric-card">
                    <h3>GPU Usage</h3>
                    <div class="metric-value" id="gpuValue">0%</div>
                    <div class="metric-label"><span id="gpuName">No GPU</span></div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Memory: <span id="gpuMemory">0</span> / <span id="gpuMemoryTotal">0</span> GB</div>
                    <div class="metric-bar">
                        <div class="metric-bar-fill" id="gpuBar" style="width: 0%"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Disk -->
                <div class="metric-card">
                    <h3>Disk Usage</h3>
                    <div class="metric-value" id="diskValue">0%</div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Used: <span id="diskUsed">0</span> / <span id="diskTotal">0</span> GB</div>
                    <div class="metric-bar">
                        <div class="metric-bar-fill" id="diskBar" style="width: 0%"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Model Status -->
                <div class="metric-card">
                    <h3>Model Status</h3>
                    <div class="metric-label">Type: <span id="modelType">-</span></div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Classes: <span id="modelClasses">0</span></div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Device: <span id="modelDevice">-</span></div>
                    <span class="status-badge" id="modelStatus">Unknown</span>
                </div>

                <!-- Performance -->
                <div class="metric-card">
                    <h3>Performance</h3>
                    <div class="metric-label">Requests: <span id="totalRequests">0</span></div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Errors: <span id="totalErrors">0</span> (<span id="errorRate">0</span>%)</div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Avg Inference: <span id="avgInference">0</span> ms</div>
                    <div class="metric-label">Total Inferences: <span id="totalInferences">0</span></div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Control Panel -->
            <div class="control-panel">
                <h2>System Controls</h2>
                <div class="button-group">
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="pingServer()">Ping Server</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="resetTracking()">Reset Tracking</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="refreshMetrics()">Refresh Metrics</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="shutdownServer()">Shutdown Server</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Camera Management View -->
        <div id="cameraManagementView" style="display: none;">
            <!-- Camera Statistics -->
            <div class="camera-stats">
                <div class="camera-stat-card">
                    <div class="camera-stat-value" id="totalCameras">0</div>
                    <div class="camera-stat-label">Total Cameras</div>
                </div>
                <div class="camera-stat-card">
                    <div class="camera-stat-value" id="onlineCameras">0</div>
                    <div class="camera-stat-label">Online</div>
                </div>
                <div class="camera-stat-card">
                    <div class="camera-stat-value" id="offlineCameras">0</div>
                    <div class="camera-stat-label">Offline</div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Broadcast Controls -->
            <div class="control-panel">
                <h2>Broadcast Controls</h2>
                <div class="button-group">
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showBroadcastModal()">Broadcast Text Message</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" id="broadcastVoiceBtn" onclick="toggleBroadcastVoice()">Broadcast Voice</button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Camera Grid -->
            <div id="cameraGridContainer">
                <div class="camera-grid" id="cameraGrid">
                    <!-- Camera cards will be inserted here -->
                </div>
                <div id="emptyCamerasState" class="empty-state" style="display: none;">
                    <div class="empty-state-icon">📷</div>
                    <h3>No Cameras Connected</h3>
                    <p>Cameras will appear here when users connect to the system</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="last-update">
            Auto-refresh every 5 seconds
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Message Modal -->
    <div id="messageModal" class="modal">
        <div class="modal-content">
            <div class="modal-header">
                <h2 class="modal-title" id="modalTitle">Send Message</h2>
                <button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">&times;</button>
            </div>
            <textarea
                id="messageInput"
                class="message-input"
                placeholder="Enter your message here. It will be announced via text-to-speech on the camera."
            ></textarea>
            <div class="button-group">
                <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="sendMessage()">Send Message</button>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Camera View Modal -->
    <div id="cameraViewModal" class="modal" onclick="if(event.target === this) closeCameraViewModal()">
        <div class="modal-content" style="max-width: 90vw; max-height: 90vh; overflow-y: auto; width: 800px;">
            <div class="modal-header">
                <h2 class="modal-title" id="cameraViewTitle">Camera View</h2>
                <button class="modal-close" onclick="closeCameraViewModal()">&times;</button>
            </div>
            <div id="cameraViewContent">
                <div id="cameraStreamContainer" style="position: relative; width: 100%; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3); border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden; margin-bottom: 15px; max-height: 60vh;">
                    <img id="cameraStreamImage" style="width: 100%; height: auto; display: block; object-fit: contain;" alt="Camera Stream">
                    <div id="cameraStreamStatus" style="position: absolute; top: 10px; left: 10px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.7); color: white; padding: 8px 12px; border-radius: 5px; font-size: 0.9rem; z-index: 10;">
                        Loading...
                    </div>
                    <div id="noStreamMessage" style="display: none; padding: 60px 20px; text-align: center; color: var(--text-secondary);">
                        <p style="font-size: 1.1rem; margin-bottom: 10px;">Camera Streaming Disabled</p>
                        <p style="font-size: 0.9rem;">User has disabled camera streaming to dashboard</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); gap: 10px;">
                    <div class="detail-row">
                        <span class="label">Camera Name:</span>
                        <span class="value" id="viewCameraName">-</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-row">
                        <span class="label">Status:</span>
                        <span class="value" id="viewCameraStatus">-</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-row">
                        <span class="label">FPS:</span>
                        <span class="value" id="viewCameraFPS">-</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-row">
                        <span class="label">Streaming:</span>
                        <span class="value" id="viewStreamingStatus">-</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script type="module" src="/static/js/modules/dashboard.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Toggle broadcast voice button
        function toggleBroadcastVoice() {
            const btn = document.getElementById('broadcastVoiceBtn');
            if (btn.textContent === 'Broadcast Voice') {
                window.startBroadcastVoice();
            } else {
                window.stopBroadcastVoice();
            }
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
#+end_src
*** index.html
#+begin_src html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <meta name="description" content="Indoor Navigation System for the Visually Impaired using computer vision and voice commands">
    <title>Blind Navigation System - Object Detection</title>
    <style>
        @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Poppins:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800;900&display=swap');
        
        :root {
            --primary-gradient: linear-gradient(135deg, #6366f1 0%, #8b5cf6 25%, #d946ef 50%, #f59e0b 75%, #ef4444 100%);
            --secondary-gradient: linear-gradient(135deg, #1e293b 0%, #334155 100%);
            --accent-gradient: linear-gradient(135deg, #06b6d4 0%, #3b82f6 100%);
            --glass-bg: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
            --glass-border: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
            --text-primary: #ffffff;
            --text-secondary: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8);
            --shadow-primary: 0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
            --shadow-glow: 0 0 40px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3);
        }

        * {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        body {
            font-family: 'Poppins', sans-serif;
            background: #0f0f23;
            min-height: 100vh;
            overflow-x: hidden;
            position: relative;
        }

        body::before {
            content: '';
            position: fixed;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background: var(--primary-gradient);
            opacity: 0.1;
            z-index: 1;
        }

        /* Enhanced animated background */
        .particles {
            position: fixed;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            pointer-events: none;
            z-index: 2;
        }

        .particle {
            position: absolute;
            background: linear-gradient(45deg, #6366f1, #8b5cf6);
            border-radius: 50%;
            filter: blur(1px);
            animation: float 8s ease-in-out infinite;
            box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4);
        }

        @keyframes float {
            0%, 100% { 
                transform: translateY(0px) translateX(0px) rotate(0deg) scale(1);
                opacity: 0.8;
            }
            25% { 
                transform: translateY(-30px) translateX(20px) rotate(90deg) scale(1.2);
                opacity: 1;
            }
            50% { 
                transform: translateY(-60px) translateX(-10px) rotate(180deg) scale(0.8);
                opacity: 0.6;
            }
            75% { 
                transform: translateY(-20px) translateX(-30px) rotate(270deg) scale(1.1);
                opacity: 0.9;
            }
        }

        .particle:nth-child(1) { width: 6px; height: 6px; left: 5%; top: 20%; animation-delay: 0s; }
        .particle:nth-child(2) { width: 10px; height: 10px; left: 15%; top: 60%; animation-delay: -1.2s; }
        .particle:nth-child(3) { width: 4px; height: 4px; left: 25%; top: 80%; animation-delay: -2.4s; }
        .particle:nth-child(4) { width: 8px; height: 8px; left: 35%; top: 40%; animation-delay: -3.6s; }
        .particle:nth-child(5) { width: 12px; height: 12px; left: 45%; top: 70%; animation-delay: -4.8s; }
        .particle:nth-child(6) { width: 7px; height: 7px; left: 55%; top: 30%; animation-delay: -6s; }
        .particle:nth-child(7) { width: 9px; height: 9px; left: 65%; top: 85%; animation-delay: -7.2s; }
        .particle:nth-child(8) { width: 5px; height: 5px; left: 75%; top: 50%; animation-delay: -8.4s; }
        .particle:nth-child(9) { width: 11px; height: 11px; left: 85%; top: 15%; animation-delay: -9.6s; }
        .particle:nth-child(10) { width: 6px; height: 6px; left: 95%; top: 75%; animation-delay: -10.8s; }

        /* Header with project details */
        .header {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(24px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 24px;
            margin: clamp(12px, 3vw, 24px);
            padding: clamp(20px, 4vw, 32px);
            z-index: 10;
            position: relative;
            transform: translateY(-30px);
            opacity: 0;
            animation: slideInDown 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1) 0.3s forwards;
            transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94);
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-primary);
        }

        .header:hover {
            transform: translateY(-8px);
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-glow);
            border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4);
        }

        @keyframes slideInDown {
            to {
                transform: translateY(0);
                opacity: 1;
            }
        }

        .header-content {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr auto;
            gap: clamp(16px, 4vw, 32px);
            align-items: center;
        }

        .project-info {
            color: var(--text-primary);
        }

        .project-title {
            font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 5vw, 3.5rem);
            font-weight: 800;
            background: var(--primary-gradient);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            background-clip: text;
            margin-bottom: clamp(8px, 2vw, 16px);
            letter-spacing: -0.02em;
            line-height: 1.1;
            filter: drop-shadow(0 0 20px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3));
        }

        .project-details {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(200px, 100%), 1fr));
            gap: clamp(12px, 2.5vw, 20px);
            margin-top: clamp(12px, 3vw, 20px);
        }

        .detail-item {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            padding: clamp(12px, 3vw, 18px);
            border-radius: 16px;
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94);
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
        }

        .detail-item::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: -100%;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.1), transparent);
            transition: left 0.6s ease;
        }

        .detail-item:hover::before {
            left: 100%;
        }

        .detail-item:hover {
            background: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.1);
            transform: translateY(-4px) scale(1.02);
            border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3);
            box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
        }

        .detail-label {
            font-weight: 500;
            opacity: 0.7;
            font-size: clamp(0.8rem, 2vw, 0.9rem);
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
        }

        .detail-value {
            font-size: clamp(1rem, 2.5vw, 1.2rem);
            font-weight: 700;
            margin-top: 6px;
            background: linear-gradient(45deg, #ffffff, #e2e8f0);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            background-clip: text;
        }

        .toggle-btn {
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            border: none;
            color: white;
            padding: clamp(10px, 2.5vw, 16px) clamp(20px, 4vw, 32px);
            border-radius: 16px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: clamp(0.9rem, 2vw, 1rem);
            transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94);
            box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.3);
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
        }

        .toggle-btn::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: -100%;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2), transparent);
            transition: left 0.5s ease;
        }

        .toggle-btn:hover::before {
            left: 100%;
        }

        .toggle-btn:hover {
            transform: translateY(-3px) scale(1.05);
            box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.4);
        }

        .toggle-btn:active {
            transform: translateY(-1px) scale(1.02);
        }

        /* Main content */
        .container {
            max-width: 1600px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            padding: clamp(12px, 3vw, 24px);
            z-index: 10;
            position: relative;
        }

        .main-content {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(400px, 100%), 1fr));
            gap: clamp(20px, 4vw, 40px);
            margin-top: clamp(16px, 3vw, 24px);
        }

        .camera-section, .controls-section {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(24px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 24px;
            padding: clamp(20px, 4vw, 40px);
            transform: translateY(30px);
            opacity: 0;
            animation: slideInUp 1s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1) forwards;
            transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94);
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-primary);
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
        }

        .camera-section::before, .controls-section::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            right: 0;
            height: 1px;
            background: var(--primary-gradient);
            opacity: 0.6;
        }

        .camera-section {
            animation-delay: 0.5s;
        }

        .controls-section {
            animation-delay: 0.7s;
        }

        .camera-section:hover, .controls-section:hover {
            transform: translateY(-8px);
            box-shadow: var(--shadow-glow);
            border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4);
        }

        @keyframes slideInUp {
            to {
                transform: translateY(0);
                opacity: 1;
            }
        }

        .section-title {
            font-size: clamp(1.4rem, 4vw, 2.2rem);
            font-weight: 700;
            background: var(--primary-gradient);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            background-clip: text;
            margin-bottom: clamp(20px, 4vw, 32px);
            text-align: center;
            position: relative;
            letter-spacing: -0.01em;
        }

        .section-title::after {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            bottom: -12px;
            left: 50%;
            transform: translateX(-50%);
            width: 80px;
            height: 4px;
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            border-radius: 2px;
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.5);
        }

        /* Camera display with enhanced design */
        .camera-container {
            position: relative;
            border-radius: 20px;
            overflow: hidden;
            background: #000;
            margin-bottom: clamp(16px, 3vw, 24px);
            box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
            aspect-ratio: 16/9;
        }

        #videoElement, #detectionCanvas {
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            object-fit: cover;
            border-radius: 20px;
        }

        #detectionCanvas {
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            z-index: 2;
        }

        .camera-overlay {
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            right: 0;
            bottom: 0;
            background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.1) 0%, transparent 50%);
            pointer-events: none;
            border-radius: 20px;
            border: 2px solid transparent;
            background-clip: padding-box;
        }

        /* Real-time detection indicator */
        .realtime-indicator {
            position: absolute;
            top: 16px;
            right: 16px;
            background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.9);
            color: white;
            padding: 6px 12px;
            border-radius: 20px;
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            display: none;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 6px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
            z-index: 10;
        }

        .realtime-indicator.active {
            display: flex;
            animation: fadeInScale 0.5s ease-out;
        }

        .realtime-dot {
            width: 8px;
            height: 8px;
            background: white;
            border-radius: 50%;
            animation: pulse 1.5s infinite;
        }

        @keyframes fadeInScale {
            from {
                opacity: 0;
                transform: scale(0.8);
            }
            to {
                opacity: 1;
                transform: scale(1);
            }
        }

        /* Enhanced control buttons */
        .btn-group {
            display: flex;
            flex-wrap: wrap;
            gap: clamp(12px, 2.5vw, 20px);
            justify-content: center;
            margin-bottom: clamp(20px, 4vw, 32px);
        }

        .btn {
            background: var(--secondary-gradient);
            border: 2px solid var(--glass-border);
            color: var(--text-primary);
            padding: clamp(12px, 2.5vw, 18px) clamp(20px, 4vw, 32px);
            border-radius: 16px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: clamp(0.85rem, 2vw, 1rem);
            transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94);
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
            box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.3);
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            min-width: 120px;
        }

        .btn::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: -100%;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background: var(--primary-gradient);
            transition: left 0.5s ease;
            z-index: -1;
        }

        .btn:hover::before {
            left: 0;
        }

        .btn:hover {
            transform: translateY(-4px) scale(1.05);
            box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4);
            border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.6);
        }

        .btn:active {
            transform: translateY(-2px) scale(1.02);
        }

        .btn.active {
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            border-color: rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.6);
            box-shadow: 0 12px 30px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.4);
        }

        .btn:disabled {
            opacity: 0.5;
            cursor: not-allowed;
            transform: none !important;
        }

        /* Status indicator with pulsing animation */
        .status-indicator {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: clamp(8px, 2vw, 12px);
            margin-bottom: clamp(16px, 3vw, 24px);
            padding: clamp(12px, 2.5vw, 18px);
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            border-radius: 16px;
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
        }

        .status-dot {
            width: 14px;
            height: 14px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: #ef4444;
            animation: pulse 2s infinite;
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.5);
        }

        .status-dot.active {
            background: #10b981;
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.5);
        }

        .status-dot.detecting {
            background: #f59e0b;
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.5);
            animation: detectingPulse 1s infinite;
        }

        @keyframes pulse {
            0%, 100% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; }
            50% { transform: scale(1.3); opacity: 0.7; }
        }

        @keyframes detectingPulse {
            0%, 100% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; }
            25% { transform: scale(1.2); opacity: 0.8; }
            50% { transform: scale(1.4); opacity: 0.6; }
            75% { transform: scale(1.2); opacity: 0.8; }
        }

        .status-text {
            color: var(--text-primary);
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: clamp(0.9rem, 2vw, 1rem);
        }

        /* Enhanced statistics panel */
        .stats-panel {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(100px, 1fr));
            gap: clamp(12px, 2.5vw, 20px);
            margin-top: clamp(16px, 3vw, 24px);
        }

        .stat-item {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            padding: clamp(16px, 3vw, 24px);
            border-radius: 20px;
            text-align: center;
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94);
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
        }

        .stat-item::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            right: 0;
            bottom: 0;
            background: var(--primary-gradient);
            opacity: 0;
            transition: opacity 0.3s ease;
        }

        .stat-item:hover::before {
            opacity: 0.1;
        }

        .stat-item:hover {
            transform: translateY(-6px) scale(1.05);
            border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4);
            box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3);
        }

        .stat-value {
            font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 4vw, 2.5rem);
            font-weight: 800;
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            background-clip: text;
            display: block;
            position: relative;
            z-index: 1;
        }

        .stat-label {
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            font-size: clamp(0.75rem, 1.8vw, 0.9rem);
            margin-top: 8px;
            font-weight: 500;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
            position: relative;
            z-index: 1;
        }

        /* Detection results with modern design */
        .detection-results {
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            border-radius: 20px;
            padding: clamp(16px, 3vw, 24px);
            margin-top: clamp(16px, 3vw, 24px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            max-height: 300px;
            overflow-y: auto;
            scrollbar-width: thin;
            scrollbar-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.5) transparent;
        }

        .detection-results::-webkit-scrollbar {
            width: 6px;
        }

        .detection-results::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
            background: transparent;
        }

        .detection-results::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
            background: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.5);
            border-radius: 3px;
        }

        .detection-item {
            background: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.1);
            margin: clamp(8px, 2vw, 12px) 0;
            padding: clamp(12px, 2.5vw, 18px);
            border-radius: 16px;
            border-left: 4px solid;
            border-image: var(--accent-gradient) 1;
            transform: translateX(-30px);
            opacity: 0;
            animation: slideInRight 0.6s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1) forwards;
            transition: all 0.3s ease;
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
        }

        .detection-item::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: -100%;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.1), transparent);
            transition: left 0.6s ease;
        }

        .detection-item:hover::before {
            left: 100%;
        }

        .detection-item:hover {
            transform: translateY(-2px) scale(1.02);
            background: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
        }

        @keyframes slideInRight {
            to {
                transform: translateX(0);
                opacity: 1;
            }
        }

        .detection-class {
            font-weight: 700;
            background: var(--accent-gradient);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            background-clip: text;
            font-size: clamp(1rem, 2.2vw, 1.2rem);
            text-transform: capitalize;
        }

        .detection-confidence {
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            margin-top: 6px;
            font-size: clamp(0.85rem, 2vw, 0.95rem);
            font-weight: 500;
        }

        /* Responsive design improvements */
        @media (max-width: 768px) {
            .main-content {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
                gap: clamp(16px, 4vw, 24px);
            }
            
            .header-content {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
                text-align: center;
                gap: clamp(16px, 4vw, 24px);
            }
            
            .project-details {
                grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(150px, 100%), 1fr));
            }
            
            .btn-group {
                flex-direction: column;
                align-items: center;
            }

            .btn {
                width: 100%;
                max-width: 280px;
            }

            .stats-panel {
                grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
            }

            .particles {
                display: none;
            }
            
            .camera-container {
                aspect-ratio: 4/3;
            }
        }

        @media (max-width: 480px) {
            .project-details {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }

            .stats-panel {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }

        /* Hidden class for toggle functionality */
        .hidden {
            display: none !important;
        }

        /* Enhanced loading animation */
        .loading {
            display: inline-block;
            width: 18px;
            height: 18px;
            border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
            border-radius: 50%;
            border-top-color: #fff;
            animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite;
            margin-right: 8px;
        }

        @keyframes spin {
            to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
        }

        /* Voice message indicator */
        .voice-indicator {
            position: fixed;
            top: 20px;
            left: 50%;
            transform: translateX(-50%);
            z-index: 9999;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #06b6d4 0%, #3b82f6 100%);
            color: white;
            padding: clamp(12px, 3vw, 16px) clamp(20px, 4vw, 32px);
            border-radius: 50px;
            display: none;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 12px;
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: clamp(0.9rem, 2vw, 1rem);
            box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.4);
            animation: voicePulse 1.5s ease-in-out infinite;
            backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
        }

        .voice-indicator-icon {
            width: 24px;
            height: 24px;
            background: white;
            border-radius: 50%;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            font-size: 14px;
            animation: voiceIconPulse 0.8s ease-in-out infinite;
        }

        @keyframes voicePulse {
            0%, 100% {
                box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.4);
            }
            50% {
                box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.6);
            }
        }

        @keyframes voiceIconPulse {
            0%, 100% {
                transform: scale(1);
            }
            50% {
                transform: scale(1.2);
            }
        }

        /* Enhanced glow effects */
        .camera-container.recording {
            animation: recordingGlow 2s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
        }

        @keyframes recordingGlow {
            from { 
                box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
                border: 2px solid transparent;
            }
            to { 
                box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.5);
                border: 2px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
            }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <!-- Animated background particles -->
    <div class="particles">
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
        <div class="particle"></div>
    </div>

    <!-- Header with project information -->
    <header class="header" id="projectHeader" role="banner" aria-label="Project information header">
        <div class="header-content">
            <div class="project-info">
                <h1 class="project-title">Indoor Navigation System for the Visually Impaired</h1>
                <div class="project-details" role="list" aria-label="Project details">
                    <div class="detail-item" role="listitem">
                        <div class="detail-label">Course Name</div>
                        <div class="detail-value">Computer Vision</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-item" role="listitem">
                        <div class="detail-label">Course Code</div>
                        <div class="detail-value">UCSC-316L</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-item" role="listitem">
                        <div class="detail-label">Guide Name</div>
                        <div class="detail-value">Dr. Shunmuga Perumal</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-item" role="listitem">
                        <div class="detail-label">Team Lead</div>
                        <div class="detail-value">Parikshieth Harish (23BCA0127)</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="detail-item" role="listitem">
                        <div class="detail-label">Team Members</div>
                        <div class="detail-value">Sakthivel M (23BCA0092),<br> Chetak Kumar Mahato (23BCA0105)</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <button class="toggle-btn" onclick="toggleHeader()" aria-label="Toggle project details visibility" aria-pressed="false">
                <span id="toggleText">Hide Details</span>
            </button>
        </div>
    </header>

    <!-- Voice message indicator -->
    <div class="voice-indicator" id="voiceIndicator" role="status" aria-live="assertive">
        <div class="voice-indicator-icon">🎙️</div>
        <span>Dashboard operator is speaking...</span>
    </div>

    <div class="container">
        <div class="main-content" role="main">
            <!-- Camera Section -->
            <section class="camera-section" aria-labelledby="cameraTitle" role="region">
                <h2 class="section-title" id="cameraTitle">Live Camera Feed</h2>

                <div class="status-indicator" role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
                    <div class="status-dot" id="statusDot" aria-hidden="true"></div>
                    <span class="status-text" id="statusText">Camera Disconnected</span>
                </div>

                <div class="camera-container" id="cameraContainer" role="img" aria-label="Live camera feed with object detection overlay">
                    <video id="videoElement" autoplay muted playsinline style="display: none;" aria-label="Camera video stream"></video>
                    <canvas id="detectionCanvas" style="display: none;" aria-label="Object detection visualization layer"></canvas>
                    <div class="camera-overlay" aria-hidden="true"></div>
                    <div class="realtime-indicator" id="realtimeIndicator" role="status" aria-live="off">
                        <div class="realtime-dot" aria-hidden="true"></div>
                        LIVE DETECTION
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="btn-group" role="toolbar" aria-label="Camera and detection controls">
                    <button class="btn" id="startBtn" onclick="startCamera()" aria-label="Start camera and object detection">
                        <span id="startBtnText">Start Camera & Detection</span>
                    </button>
                    <button class="btn" id="audioBtn" onclick="toggleAudio()" aria-label="Toggle audio announcements on or off" aria-pressed="true">
                        <span id="audioBtnText">🔊 Audio ON</span>
                    </button>
                    <button class="btn" id="boxesBtn" onclick="toggleDetectionBoxes()" aria-label="Toggle detection boxes visibility" aria-pressed="true">
                        <span id="boxesBtnText">📦 Boxes ON</span>
                    </button>
                    <button class="btn" id="testAudioBtn" onclick="testAudio()" aria-label="Test audio system">
                        <span>🧪 Test Audio</span>
                    </button>
                    <button class="btn" id="testStatsBtn" onclick="testStats()" aria-label="Test statistics display">
                        <span>📊 Test Stats</span>
                    </button>
                    <button onclick="testConnection()" class="btn" aria-label="Test connection to server">🔌 Test Connection</button>
                    <button onclick="showOrientationDebug()" class="btn" aria-label="Show orientation debugging information">🔍 Debug Orientation</button>
                    <button onclick="testGyroscope()" class="btn" aria-label="Test device gyroscope">🧪 Test Gyroscope</button>
                    <button onclick="requestOrientationPermission()" class="btn" aria-label="Request permission to access device orientation">📱 Enable Rotation</button>
                    <button onclick="togglePathfinding()" class="btn" id="pathfindingBtn" aria-label="Toggle pathfinding feature" aria-pressed="false">🗺️ Enable Pathfinding</button>
                </div>
            </section>

            <!-- Controls and Results Section -->
            <section class="controls-section" aria-labelledby="resultsTitle" role="region">
                <h2 class="section-title" id="resultsTitle">Detection Results</h2>

                <div class="stats-panel" role="list" aria-label="Detection statistics">
                    <div class="stat-item" role="listitem">
                        <span class="stat-value" id="objectCount" aria-label="Number of objects detected">0</span>
                        <span class="stat-label">Objects Detected</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="stat-item" role="listitem">
                        <span class="stat-value" id="fpsCounter" aria-label="Frames per second">0</span>
                        <span class="stat-label">FPS</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="stat-item" role="listitem">
                        <span class="stat-value" id="confidenceAvg" aria-label="Average confidence percentage">0%</span>
                        <span class="stat-label">Avg Confidence</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="stat-item" role="listitem">
                        <span class="stat-value" id="audioStatus" aria-label="Audio system status">🔊</span>
                        <span class="stat-label">Audio Status</span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="detection-results" id="detectionResults" role="log" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="false" aria-relevant="additions text" aria-label="Live detection results">
                    <p style="color: white; text-align: center; opacity: 0.7;">
                        Start detection to see results here...
                    </p>
                </div>
            </section>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script type="module" src="/static/js/main.js"></script>

    <!-- Mobile Console (Eruda) - For debugging on mobile devices -->
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/eruda"></script>
    <script>
        // Initialize Eruda mobile console
        eruda.init();
        console.log('📱 Mobile console initialized - tap the green icon to open DevTools');
    </script>
</body>
</html>

*** camera_calibration.py
#+begin_src python
"""
Camera calibration utilities using pinhole camera model.
Provides fallback distance estimation when depth models are unavailable.
"""

import numpy as np
import logging
from ..constants import DEFAULT_FOCAL_LENGTH, KNOWN_OBJECT_HEIGHTS

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class PinholeCameraModel:
    """
    Pinhole camera model for distance estimation.

    Uses the formula: distance = (known_height * focal_length) / bbox_pixel_height
    """

    def __init__(self, focal_length=None, image_width=640, image_height=480):
        """
        Initialize camera calibration.

        Args:
            focal_length: Camera focal length in pixels (auto-estimated if None)
            image_width: Image width in pixels
            image_height: Image height in pixels
        """
        self.image_width = image_width
        self.image_height = image_height

        if focal_length is None:
            # Estimate focal length assuming 60-degree horizontal FOV (typical mobile camera)
            self.focal_length = self._estimate_focal_length(image_width)
            logger.info(f"Auto-estimated focal length: {self.focal_length:.1f} pixels")
        else:
            self.focal_length = focal_length

    def _estimate_focal_length(self, image_width, horizontal_fov_degrees=60):
        """
        Estimate focal length from horizontal field of view.

        Args:
            image_width: Image width in pixels
            horizontal_fov_degrees: Horizontal field of view in degrees

        Returns:
            float: Estimated focal length in pixels
        """
        horizontal_fov_radians = np.radians(horizontal_fov_degrees)
        focal_length = image_width / (2 * np.tan(horizontal_fov_radians / 2))
        return focal_length

    def estimate_distance(self, bbox, class_name):
        """
        Estimate distance to object using pinhole camera model.

        Args:
            bbox: Bounding box in [x1, y1, x2, y2] format
            class_name: Object class name

        Returns:
            float: Estimated distance in meters, or None if cannot estimate
        """
        # Normalize class name to lowercase for case-insensitive lookup
        class_name_normalized = class_name.lower() if class_name else ''

        # Check if we have known height for this class
        if class_name_normalized not in KNOWN_OBJECT_HEIGHTS:
            logger.debug(f"No known height for class '{class_name}' - cannot estimate distance")
            return None

        known_height = KNOWN_OBJECT_HEIGHTS[class_name_normalized]

        # Calculate bbox dimensions
        x1, y1, x2, y2 = bbox
        bbox_width = x2 - x1
        bbox_height = y2 - y1

        # Sanity check
        if bbox_height <= 0:
            logger.warning(f"Invalid bbox height: {bbox_height}")
            return None

        # Apply pinhole camera formula
        # distance = (real_height * focal_length) / image_height
        distance = (known_height * self.focal_length) / bbox_height

        # Sanity check: distances should be reasonable (0.1m to 100m)
        if distance < 0.1 or distance > 100:
            logger.warning(f"Unrealistic distance estimate: {distance:.2f}m for {class_name}")
            return None

        logger.debug(f"Pinhole distance for {class_name}: {distance:.2f}m (bbox_h={bbox_height:.1f}px)")
        return distance

    def estimate_distance_with_confidence(self, bbox, class_name):
        """
        Estimate distance with confidence score.

        Args:
            bbox: Bounding box in [x1, y1, x2, y2] format
            class_name: Object class name

        Returns:
            tuple: (distance in meters, confidence score 0-1), or (None, 0.0)
        """
        distance = self.estimate_distance(bbox, class_name)

        if distance is None:
            return None, 0.0

        # Calculate confidence based on bbox quality
        x1, y1, x2, y2 = bbox
        bbox_width = x2 - x1
        bbox_height = y2 - y1
        bbox_area = bbox_width * bbox_height
        image_area = self.image_width * self.image_height

        # Confidence factors:
        # 1. Bbox should not be too small (< 1% of image) or too large (> 80%)
        area_ratio = bbox_area / image_area
        if area_ratio < 0.01:
            confidence = 0.3  # Low confidence for very small objects
        elif area_ratio > 0.8:
            confidence = 0.4  # Low confidence for very large objects (likely cropped)
        elif 0.05 <= area_ratio <= 0.5:
            confidence = 0.8  # High confidence for well-sized objects
        else:
            confidence = 0.6  # Medium confidence

        # 2. Aspect ratio should be reasonable for the object class
        aspect_ratio = bbox_width / bbox_height

        # Expected aspect ratios for common classes
        expected_aspect_ratios = {
            'person': (0.3, 0.8),  # People are taller than wide
            'car': (1.2, 2.0),  # Cars are wider than tall
            'bicycle': (0.8, 1.5),
            'motorcycle': (0.9, 1.8),
            'bus': (1.5, 3.0),
            'truck': (1.3, 2.5),
        }

        # Check aspect ratio using normalized class name
        class_name_normalized = class_name.lower() if class_name else ''
        if class_name_normalized in expected_aspect_ratios:
            min_ar, max_ar = expected_aspect_ratios[class_name_normalized]
            if min_ar <= aspect_ratio <= max_ar:
                # Good aspect ratio - no penalty
                pass
            else:
                # Bad aspect ratio - reduce confidence
                confidence *= 0.7
                logger.debug(f"Unusual aspect ratio {aspect_ratio:.2f} for {class_name}")

        return distance, confidence

    def update_image_dimensions(self, width, height):
        """
        Update image dimensions (e.g., when camera resolution changes).

        Args:
            width: New image width
            height: New image height
        """
        self.image_width = width
        self.image_height = height
        # Re-estimate focal length if it was auto-estimated
        self.focal_length = self._estimate_focal_length(width)
        logger.info(f"Updated camera dimensions to {width}x{height}, focal_length={self.focal_length:.1f}px")


# Global pinhole camera model instance
_pinhole_model = None


def get_pinhole_model(focal_length=None, image_width=640, image_height=480):
    """
    Get the global pinhole camera model instance (singleton).

    Args:
        focal_length: Camera focal length in pixels
        image_width: Image width in pixels
        image_height: Image height in pixels

    Returns:
        PinholeCameraModel: The model instance
    """
    global _pinhole_model
    if _pinhole_model is None:
        _pinhole_model = PinholeCameraModel(focal_length, image_width, image_height)
    return _pinhole_model
#+end_src

"""
Filtering utilities for object detection - blacklisting, normalization, and deduplication.
"""

import logging
from ..constants import BLACKLISTED_CLASSES, CLASS_MAPPING
from .geometry import calculate_iou

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def is_class_blacklisted(class_name):
    """
    Check if a class is in the blacklist.

    Args:
        class_name: Name of the class to check

    Returns:
        bool: True if the class is blacklisted
    """
    return class_name.lower() in BLACKLISTED_CLASSES


def normalize_class_name(class_name):
    """
    Normalize class name using mapping (e.g., man/woman/face -> person).

    Args:
        class_name: Original class name

    Returns:
        str: Normalized class name
    """
    normalized = class_name.lower()
    return CLASS_MAPPING.get(normalized, class_name)


def deduplicate_same_class_detections(detections, iou_threshold=0.5):
    """
    Remove duplicate detections of the SAME class that overlap significantly.
    For example, if "person" and "face" both map to "person" and overlap,
    keep only the one with higher confidence.

    Args:
        detections: List of detection dicts with 'class_name', 'bbox_xyxy', 'confidence'
        iou_threshold: IoU threshold for considering boxes as duplicates

    Returns:
        list: Deduplicated list of detections
    """
    if len(detections) <= 1:
        return detections

    # Group by normalized class name
    class_groups = {}
    for det in detections:
        cls = det['class_name']
        if cls not in class_groups:
            class_groups[cls] = []
        class_groups[cls].append(det)

    # Deduplicate within each class group
    deduplicated = []
    for cls, group in class_groups.items():
        # Sort by confidence (highest first)
        group_sorted = sorted(group, key=lambda x: x['confidence'], reverse=True)

        kept = []
        for det in group_sorted:
            # Check if this detection overlaps significantly with any already kept detection
            should_keep = True
            for kept_det in kept:
                iou = calculate_iou(det['bbox_xyxy'], kept_det['bbox_xyxy'])
                if iou > iou_threshold:
                    # This is a duplicate of a higher-confidence detection
                    should_keep = False
                    logger.debug(f"Removing duplicate {cls} detection (IoU={iou:.2f})")
                    break

            if should_keep:
                kept.append(det)

        deduplicated.extend(kept)

    return deduplicated


def filter_detections_by_confidence(detections, min_confidence=0.3):
    """
    Filter detections by minimum confidence threshold.

    Args:
        detections: List of detection dictionaries
        min_confidence: Minimum confidence threshold (0.0 to 1.0)

    Returns:
        list: Filtered detections
    """
    return [det for det in detections if det.get('confidence', 0) >= min_confidence]


def filter_detections_by_classes(detections, allowed_classes):
    """
    Keep only detections of specific classes.

    Args:
        detections: List of detection dictionaries
        allowed_classes: Set or list of class names to keep

    Returns:
        list: Filtered detections
    """
    allowed_set = set(cls.lower() for cls in allowed_classes)
    return [det for det in detections if det.get('class_name', '').lower() in allowed_set]
#+end_src

#+begin_src python
"""
Geometric calculations for object detection and tracking.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial.distance import euclidean


def calculate_iou(box1, box2):
    """
    Calculate Intersection over Union (IoU) between two bounding boxes.

    Args:
        box1: Bounding box in [x1, y1, x2, y2] format
        box2: Bounding box in [x1, y1, x2, y2] format

    Returns:
        float: IoU score between 0 and 1
    """
    x1_min, y1_min, x1_max, y1_max = box1
    x2_min, y2_min, x2_max, y2_max = box2

    # Calculate intersection area
    intersect_x_min = max(x1_min, x2_min)
    intersect_y_min = max(y1_min, y2_min)
    intersect_x_max = min(x1_max, x2_max)
    intersect_y_max = min(y1_max, y2_max)

    if intersect_x_max < intersect_x_min or intersect_y_max < intersect_y_min:
        return 0.0  # No intersection

    intersection = (intersect_x_max - intersect_x_min) * (intersect_y_max - intersect_y_min)

    # Calculate union area
    box1_area = (x1_max - x1_min) * (y1_max - y1_min)
    box2_area = (x2_max - x2_min) * (y2_max - y2_min)
    union = box1_area + box2_area - intersection

    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0


def calculate_distance(point1, point2):
    """
    Calculate Euclidean distance between two points.

    Args:
        point1: Tuple of (x, y) coordinates
        point2: Tuple of (x, y) coordinates

    Returns:
        float: Distance between the points
    """
    return euclidean(point1, point2)


def calculate_box_center(bbox):
    """
    Calculate the center point of a bounding box.

    Args:
        bbox: Bounding box in [x1, y1, x2, y2] format

    Returns:
        tuple: (center_x, center_y)
    """
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = bbox
    return ((x1 + x2) / 2, (y1 + y2) / 2)


def calculate_box_area(bbox):
    """
    Calculate the area of a bounding box.

    Args:
        bbox: Bounding box in [x1, y1, x2, y2] format

    Returns:
        float: Area of the bounding box
    """
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = bbox
    return (x2 - x1) * (y2 - y1)


def convert_bbox_format(bbox, from_format='xyxy', to_format='ltwh'):
    """
    Convert bounding box between different formats.

    Args:
        bbox: Bounding box coordinates
        from_format: Source format ('xyxy' or 'ltwh')
        to_format: Target format ('xyxy' or 'ltwh')

    Returns:
        list: Converted bounding box coordinates

    Formats:
        - 'xyxy': [x1, y1, x2, y2] (top-left and bottom-right corners)
        - 'ltwh': [left, top, width, height]
    """
    if from_format == to_format:
        return bbox

    if from_format == 'xyxy' and to_format == 'ltwh':
        x1, y1, x2, y2 = bbox
        return [x1, y1, x2 - x1, y2 - y1]

    elif from_format == 'ltwh' and to_format == 'xyxy':
        x, y, w, h = bbox
        return [x, y, x + w, y + h]

    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported conversion: {from_format} -> {to_format}")
#+end_src
